SCENE:
A hidden Oregon mountain kingdom gathers for a royal night festival in the castle square. The crowd looks festive from far away but frightened in close reaction shots.

SHOT PLAN:
0-3s: Title card only: FROST: SON OF THE WILD / Directed by Noorul Ain. Behind the text, a wide vertical view of L02_KingCastle at dusk, red banners moving in wind.
3-8s: Wide shot, not close-up: workers, nobles, guards, and quiet families fill the square. Show faces worried, hands gripping each other, children held back.
8-14s: King Magnus appears on the balcony in a medium-wide royal shot. Do not push camera into his face. Prince Damian stands half a step behind him, smiling politely.
14-20s: Captain Vex and guards form a path through the crowd. Rose is visible only at the side near a stone arch, half-hidden, watching with fear. She does not enter the center.
20-27s: Magnus raises one hand; the whole square becomes silent. The festival happiness feels fake, like everyone knows a secret rule.
27-30s: Final image: Captain Vex lifts a sealed royal scroll. Hold on the scroll and the silent crowd, no random extra character.

SPOKEN DIALOGUE:
DIALOGUE 1: NARRATOR (deep cinematic voice): "In Thorne Kingdom, celebration was only the mask."
DIALOGUE 2: KING MAGNUS (deep controlled voice): "Tonight, every family will hear the crown."
DIALOGUE 3: PRINCE DAMIAN (smooth polite voice): "Smile for the court. Fear makes ugly rumors."

CHARACTER LOCKS:

Exactly one Frost appears, matching V05_Frost_Baby: snow-white fur, blue eyes, Bigfoot facial structure, small rounded ears, broad humanoid hands and feet, no muzzle, canine snout, tail or paws. He is an infant Bigfoot, never a white puppy, human baby or miniature adult. Show modest rapid growth only from Part 136's explicit days passage, keeping baby proportions and the same face. Do not switch to kid, teen or adult references. No child duplicate appears.
Bruno is dead after Part 103. Do not show him alive, resurrected, or physically visiting Lily in later scenes. Memories are conveyed through Lily's behavior and his carved token unless the shot plan explicitly requests a dream.
Martha is the village helper, not Lily's mother Grace and not Rose. Hank and Old Joe retain their own referenced faces. Grace appears only in Part 113's clearly identified dream. If children appear, exactly Tommy and Sarah are the named village children; they are distinct from baby Frost. Do not add another child to Lily's household.

SHOT PLAN:
0-5s: Lily holds the loosely wrapped baby as a knock echoes from outside.
5-11s: She quietly answers that she is resting, without opening the door.
11-17s: The footsteps move away; she lowers the blanket edge to see his face.
17-24s: She says the name chosen with Bruno and holds him against her shoulder.
24-30s: Frost gives one small happy grunt, exactly one baby in her arms.

SPOKEN AUDIO:
Lily: "Hello, Frost. Your father loved you before he met you."
